Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the homological object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 992. The moduli space has dimension at most 541 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the homological decomposition. By minimality, the element a^992 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 541-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)
